# Hamsterball .MAX Files - Object & Entity Catalog

Extracted from the OLE2 Scene/SummaryInformation streams of each .MAX file.

> **Note:** Object positions (X,Y,Z coordinates) are stored in 3ds Max's proprietary
> binary Scene stream and cannot be extracted without 3ds Max itself. This catalog
> documents all names, entity types, events, materials, textures, and object names
> that were recoverable from the file metadata.

## Summary

- **Total files analyzed:** 227
- **Entities (N:/E:/T:/O:/S:):** 1477
- **Ref Points (REF: + named):** 2886
- **BADBALL tags:** 43
- **Cameras:** 291
- **Lights:** 87
- **Materials:** 970
- **Textures:** 1500
- **Bones:** 72
- **3ds Max Objects:** 2799
